# Master Inquiry — 520 Questions

Each record follows Question → Evidence → Hypothesis → Test → Result → Confidence → Decision. UNKNOWN is used where real evidence is absent.

## Market Structure

### M-Q001 — Market Structure
**Question:** Under the existence lens, what evidence would prove or disprove that spread formation improves the system?
**Evidence:** Public OHLCV is an incomplete view of matching-engine state. Engineering controls exist, but licensed real point-in-time evidence for this exact question is **UNKNOWN** unless a sealed experiment says otherwise.
**Hypothesis:** A minimal, causal implementation of spread formation adds net risk-adjusted value only in a predeclared subset of conditions.
**Test:** Freeze the definition; compare baseline versus one-component change using purged walk-forward, a sealed OOS period, realistic costs, symbol/regime decomposition, perturbation and a removal test.
**Result:** NOT_RUN_ON_PRISTINE_REAL_DATA. Existing synthetic or public-account observations are engineering/hypothesis evidence only.
**Confidence:** LOW for market edge; HIGH for the requirement to fail closed and account for selection.
**Decision:** RESEARCH_MORE. Reject the component if the lower expectancy bound is non-positive or stress/replication fails.

**Evidence:** Public OHLCV is an incomplete view of matching-engine state. Engineering controls exist, but licensed real point-in-time evidence for this exact question is **UNKNOWN** unless a sealed experiment says otherwise.
**Hypothesis:** A minimal, causal implementation of closing auction adds net risk-adjusted value only in a predeclared subset of conditions.
**Test:** Freeze the definition; compare baseline versus one-component change using purged walk-forward, a sealed OOS period, realistic costs, symbol/regime decomposition, perturbation and a removal test.
**Result:** NOT_RUN_ON_PRISTINE_REAL_DATA. Existing synthetic or public-account observations are engineering/hypothesis evidence only.
**Confidence:** LOW for market edge; HIGH for the requirement to fail closed and account for selection.
**Decision:** RESEARCH_MORE. Reject the component if the lower expectancy bound is non-positive or stress/replication fails.

**Hypothesis:** A minimal, causal implementation of portfolio heat adds net risk-adjusted value only in a predeclared subset of conditions.
**Test:** Freeze the definition; compare baseline versus one-component change using purged walk-forward, a sealed OOS period, realistic costs, symbol/regime decomposition, perturbation and a removal test.
**Result:** NOT_RUN_ON_PRISTINE_REAL_DATA. Existing synthetic or public-account observations are engineering/hypothesis evidence only.
**Confidence:** LOW for market edge; HIGH for the requirement to fail closed and account for selection.
**Decision:** RESEARCH_MORE. Reject the component if the lower expectancy bound is non-positive or stress/replication fails.

**Evidence:** A setup is not an entry until a causal trigger and executable next price exist. Engineering controls exist, but licensed real point-in-time evidence for this exact question is **UNKNOWN** unless a sealed experiment says otherwise.
**Hypothesis:** A minimal, causal implementation of next-open entry adds net risk-adjusted value only in a predeclared subset of conditions.
**Test:** Freeze the definition; compare baseline versus one-component change using purged walk-forward, a sealed OOS period, realistic costs, symbol/regime decomposition, perturbation and a removal test.
**Result:** NOT_RUN_ON_PRISTINE_REAL_DATA. Existing synthetic or public-account observations are engineering/hypothesis evidence only.
**Confidence:** LOW for market edge; HIGH for the requirement to fail closed and account for selection.
**Decision:** RESEARCH_MORE. Reject the component if the lower expectancy bound is non-positive or stress/replication fails.

## Simplicity

### M-Q451 — Simplicity
**Question:** Under the existence lens, what evidence would prove or disprove that rule removal improves the system?
**Evidence:** A component survives only if removal materially harms robust OOS evidence. Engineering controls exist, but licensed real point-in-time evidence for this exact question is **UNKNOWN** unless a sealed experiment says otherwise.

**Evidence:** Critical uncertainty stops new entries while preserving reconciliation and exits. Engineering controls exist, but licensed real point-in-time evidence for this exact question is **UNKNOWN** unless a sealed experiment says otherwise.
**Hypothesis:** A minimal, causal implementation of gap through stop adds net risk-adjusted value only in a predeclared subset of conditions.
**Test:** Freeze the definition; compare baseline versus one-component change using purged walk-forward, a sealed OOS period, realistic costs, symbol/regime decomposition, perturbation and a removal test.
**Result:** NOT_RUN_ON_PRISTINE_REAL_DATA. Existing synthetic or public-account observations are engineering/hypothesis evidence only.
**Confidence:** LOW for market edge; HIGH for the requirement to fail closed and account for selection.
**Decision:** RESEARCH_MORE. Reject the component if the lower expectancy bound is non-positive or stress/replication fails.
